# HL7 v2.5 segment validation ledger

Generated by `codegen/validate/ledger.mjs v2.5` from source tables fetched on 2026-10-25. Do not edit by hand:
rerun the script (see its header) after fixing a class, the spec, or when a source changes.

## Sources and rule

| Source | Where | Role |
|---|---|---|
| hl7.eu | `https://www.hl7.eu/HL7v2x/v25/hl7v25segm<SEG>.htm` | Authoritative |
| Caristix | `https://hl7-definition.caristix.com/v2-api/1/HL7v2.5/Segments/<SEG>` | Validation reference |
| HAPI | `hapi-structures-v25/.../model/v25/segment/<SEG>.java` field definitions | Cross-check |

For each attribute (type, repeat, usage, length) the three values are compared. When they differ, hl7.eu
decides, unless Caristix and HAPI agree with each other against it (then they decide and the row is marked
**overruled**). Every disagreement is written down in the field's row whichever way it went, with the
value each source gave. Legacy datatype names (CN/XCN, TN/XTN, AD/XAD, LA1/LA2) are treated as the same type.

Columns: **Type / Rep / Usage / Len** are the decided values. **Ours** is what the class implements:
the setter names, `rep` when the setter takes repetitions, `typed` when it takes named components,
`spec` when the class is generated from `codegen/spec/<version>-segments.json`. **Class** names the version
whose class v2.5 uses (a re-export of an older version's class, or its own). **Status** lists open
issues (our class or spec differs from the decided value), remarks, and dated hand-written notes from
`codegen/validate/ledger-v25-notes.json`; **Sources** lists the contradictions between the sources.

## Summary

- 150 segments, 2095 field rows.
- 0 rows where our implementation differs from the decided value (0 segments).
- 4 rows where the sources disagree (4 segments); each is spelled out in the segment's table.
- 53 segments have their own v2.5 class; the rest re-export an older version's.
- Sources missing for: CON (hapi).

Tests: **pos** = field-position tests exist, **n tests** = hand-written tests of the segment, **rt** = a parse/encode
round trip exists (`roundtrip.test.ts` runs one for every class), **spec** = the class is generated and
`generated.test.ts` checks every setter, repetition and composite against the spec. The last column counts
setters of hand-written classes that no hand-written test anywhere calls (the position tests still cover them;
each segment's section lists them).
